## Overview

Dr. Christopher I. Shaffrey is an orthopedist in Durham, North Carolina and is affiliated with [Duke University Hospital](https://health.usnews.com/best-hospitals/area/nc/duke-university-medical-center-6360355). He received his medical degree from University of Virginia School of Medicine and has been in practice for more than 20 years. Dr. Christopher I. Shaffrey has expertise in treating spinal fusion, spinal stenosis and spondylosis, among other conditions - see all areas of [expertise](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/christopher-shaffrey-11314#expertise). Dr. Christopher I. Shaffrey accepts Aetna, Cigna, Blue Cross - see other [insurance plans accepted](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/christopher-shaffrey-11314#insurance). Dr. Christopher I. Shaffrey is highly recommended by patients.

Doctor's Details

Speaks **English**

Works at **Duke University Hospital**

## Specialties

Specialty

### Orthopedics

Orthopedic surgeons diagnose and treat conditions of the muscles, bones and joints. They often treat sports injuries, degenerative diseases, tumors, infections and birth defects. They also perform joint repair and replacement surgeries.

Subspecialties

### Orthopedic Spine Surgery

### What are Dr. Shaffrey's  areas of care?

Orthopedic surgeons diagnose and treat conditions of the muscles, bones and joints. They often treat sports injuries, degenerative diseases, tumors, infections and birth defects. They also perform joint repair and replacement surgeries.
